I'm not making it up.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your belief about your husband's sister is something that might not be true, or is it something you're absolutely certain about?\n\nPatient: I am absolutely certain. I mean, like I keep saying, I know what I've been seeing for more than a year now. Folks can try to smooth it over all they want, but... it doesn't change what's happening. She is working that family business just so she can get control of the money and push me out of the decisions. I don't have any doubts about it at all.\n\nDoctor: Has your belief about your husband's sister trying to push you out of the family business decisions ever changed since you first started thinking it?\n\nPatient: No, it hasn't changed. If anything, I'm just more sure of it now. Like I said, I know what I've been seeing for more than a year now. She's been working this family business just so she can get control of the money and push me out of the decisions. No matter how folks try to smooth it over, I know what's going on.\n\nDoctor: Has your belief about your husband's sister affected your daily activities or relationships outside of the family business?\n\nPatient: No, not really. My regular day-to-day stuff is fine. I mean, I still keep up with my quilting group, and choir practice... I do my GED lessons and go on my senior walks just like I always have. It hasn't stopped my normal life. \n\nMostly, this whole thing has just changed how much I trust my husband's sister, and... well, which family gatherings I actually feel comfortable going to now. I just stay away if I know she's going to be causing trouble. But outside of that, everything else is normal.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever thought that your husband's sister might not actually be trying to push you out of the family business decisions?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't. I mean, I'm here talking to you because of it. My husband's sister has been working this family business just so she can get control of the money and push me out of the decisions. Like I've said, no matter how much folks try to smooth it over, I know what I've been seeing for more than a year now. I haven't ever thought otherwise because it's just what's happening.\n\nDoctor: Do you think there's any evidence that could change your mind about your husband's sister trying to push you out of the family business decisions?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. I mean, I came here to talk to you because of it, but... there's no evidence that could change my mind. My husband's sister has been working this family business so she can get control of the money and push me out of decisions. And no matter how folks try to smooth it over, I know what I've been seeing for more than a year now. It's just the truth of what's happening.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever considered that your husband's sister might actually care about the family business and is not trying to push you out?\n\nPatient: Doctor, I feel like I just keep saying the same thing to you, but no, I haven't. \n\nPeople can try to smooth it over all they want and say she means well, but I've been watching this happen for over a year now. I know what I'm seeing. She's just working the family business so she can get total control of the money and push me out of making any decisions. That's the truth of it, and it's the whole reason I came in here to talk to you today.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your belief about your husband's sister could be considered an exaggeration or distortion of reality by others?\n\nPatient: I suppose other people might see it that way, sure. Like I said, folks are always trying to smooth it over and tell me I'm wrong. But I know what I've been seeing for over a year now. I came here because she's working this family business just to get control of the money and push me out of the decisions. That's just what's happening, whether other people think it's an exaggeration or not.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your husband's sister would admit to trying to push you out of the family business decisions if you asked her directly?\n\nPatient: No, I highly doubt it. Why would she? If I asked her directly, she'd just deny it and probably act like I'm crazy for even bringing it up. People who are doing things behind your back don't just admit to it when you ask them. So no, I wouldn't expect her to tell the truth about it.\n\nDoctor: Has there ever been a time when you found evidence that contradicted your belief about your husband's sister, but you still held on to that belief?\n\nPatient: Well, no. I haven't seen anything that actually proves me wrong. \n\nI know I'm repeating myself here, but no matter how folks try to smooth it over or make excuses for her, I know what I've been seeing for more than a year now. I came in because my husband's sister has been working this family business so she can get control of the money and push me out of decisions. I haven't found any evidence that contradicts that, because that is exactly what she's doing.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your belief about your husband's sister could be false?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think it could be false. I know I keep saying the exact same thing to you, but it's just the truth. I came in here because my husband's sister has been working this family business so she can get control of the money and push me out of the decisions. No matter how much folks try to smooth it over, I know what I've been seeing for more than a year now. So no, I don't believe I'm wrong about this.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. What brings you in to see me today?\n\nPatient: Well, I came in because... it's about my husband's sister. She’s been working this family business just so she can get control of the money and push me out of all the decisions. It's been going on for more than a year now.\n\nDoctor: Do you think other people share your concerns about your husband's sister?\n\nPatient: Well, I'm not really sure. I haven't gone around asking the rest of the family about it. I just know how it's been making me feel, but... I guess I haven't noticed anyone else saying anything. Maybe they don't see it the same way, or maybe they just don't want to get involved. Like I said a minute ago, that's exactly what she's doing. She wants control of the money, and she wants me out of the decisions. People try to smooth it over and act like she's just helping out with the business, but... well, I know what I've been seeing for over a year now. I'm not making it up.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your belief about your husband's sister is something that might not be true, or is it something you're absolutely certain about?\n\nPatient: I am absolutely certain. I mean, like I keep saying, I know what I've been seeing for more than a year now. Folks can try to smooth it over all they want, but... it doesn't change what's happening. She is working that family business just so she can get control of the money and push me out of the decisions. I don't have any doubts about it at all.\n\nDoctor: Has your belief about your husband's sister trying to push you out of the family business decisions ever changed since you first started thinking it?\n\nPatient: No, it hasn't changed. If anything, I'm just more sure of it now. Like I said, I know what I've been seeing for more than a year now. She's been working this family business just so she can get control of the money and push me out of the decisions. No matter how folks try to smooth it over, I know what's going on.\n\nDoctor: Has your belief about your husband's sister affected your daily activities or relationships outside of the family business?\n\nPatient: No, not really. My regular day-to-day stuff is fine. I mean, I still keep up with my quilting group, and choir practice... I do my GED lessons and go on my senior walks just like I always have. It hasn't stopped my normal life. \n\nMostly, this whole thing has just changed how much I trust my husband's sister, and... well, which family gatherings I actually feel comfortable going to now. I just stay away if I know she's going to be causing trouble. But outside of that, everything else is normal.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever thought that your husband's sister might not actually be trying to push you out of the family business decisions?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't. My husband's sister has been working this family business so she can get control of the money and push me out of decisions. And no matter how folks try to smooth it over, I know what I've been seeing for more than a year now. It's just the truth of what's happening.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever considered that your husband's sister might actually care about the family business and is not trying to push you out?\n\nPatient: Doctor, I feel like I just keep saying the same thing to you, but no, I haven't. \n\nPeople can try to smooth it over all they want and say she means well, but I've been watching this happen for over a year now. She’s been working this family business just so she can get control of the money and push me out of all the decisions. It's been going on for more than a year now.\n\nDoctor: Do you think other people share your concerns about your husband's sister?\n\nPatient: Well, I'm not really sure. I haven't gone around asking the rest of the family about it. I just know how it's been making me feel, but... I guess I haven't noticed anyone else saying anything. Maybe they don't see it the same way, or maybe they just don't want to get involved. Like I said a minute ago, that's exactly what she's doing. She wants control of the money, and she wants me out of the decisions. People try to smooth it over and act like she's just helping out with the business, but... well, I know what I've been seeing for over a year now. I'm not making it up.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your belief about your husband's sister is something that might not be true, or is it something you're absolutely certain about?\n\nPatient: I am absolutely certain. I mean, like I keep saying, I know what I've been seeing for more than a year now. Folks can try to smooth it over all they want, but... it doesn't change what's happening. She is working that family business just so she can get control of the money and push me out of the decisions. I don't have any doubts about it at all.\n\nDoctor: Has your belief about your husband's sister trying to push you out of the family business decisions ever changed since you first started thinking it?\n\nPatient: No, it hasn't changed. If anything, I'm just more sure of it now. Like I said, I know what I've been seeing for more than a year now. She's been working this family business just so she can get control of the money and push me out of the decisions. No matter how folks try to smooth it over, I know what's going on.\n\nDoctor: Has your belief about your husband's sister affected your daily activities or relationships outside of the family business?\n\nPatient: No, not really. My regular day-to-day stuff is fine. I mean, I still keep up with my quilting group, and choir practice... I do my GED lessons and go on my senior walks just like I always have. It hasn't stopped my normal life. \n\nMostly, this whole thing has just changed how much I trust my husband's sister, and... well, which family gatherings I actually feel comfortable going to now. I just stay away if I know she's going to be causing trouble. But outside of that, everything else is normal.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever thought that your husband's sister might not actually be trying to push you out of the family business decisions?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't. I mean, I'm here talking to you because of it. My husband's sister has been working this family business just so she can get control of the money and push me out of the decisions. Like I've said, no matter how much folks try to smooth it over, I know what I've been seeing for more than a year now. I haven't ever thought otherwise because it's just what's happening.\n\nDoctor: Do you think there's any evidence that could change your mind about your husband's sister trying to push you out of the family business decisions?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. I mean, I came here to talk to you because of it, but... there's no evidence that could change my mind. My husband's sister has been working this family business so she can get control of the money and push me out of decisions. And no matter how folks try to smooth it over, I know what I've been seeing for more than a year now. It's just the truth of what's happening.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever considered that your husband's sister might actually care about the family business and is not trying to push you out?\n\nPatient: Doctor, I feel like I just keep saying the same thing to you, but no, I haven't. \n\nPeople can try to smooth it over all they want and say she means well, but I've been watching this happen for over a year now. I know what I'm seeing. She's just working the family business so she can get total control of the money and push me out of making any decisions. Like I said, folks are always trying to smooth it over and tell me I'm wrong. But I know what I've been seeing for over a year now. I came here because she's working this family business just to get control of the money and push me out of the decisions. People who are doing things behind your back don't just admit to it when you ask them. So no, I wouldn't expect her to tell the truth about it.\n\nDoctor: Has there ever been a time when you found evidence that contradicted your belief about your husband's sister, but you still held on to that belief?\n\nPatient: Well, no. What brings you in to see me today?\n\nPatient: Well, I came in because... it's about my husband's sister. She’s been working this family business just so she can get control of the money and push me out of all the decisions. And no matter how folks try to smooth it over, I know exactly what I’ve been seeing. It's been going on for more than a year now.\n\nDoctor: Do you think other people share your concerns about your husband's sister?\n\nPatient: Well, I'm not really sure. I haven't gone around asking the rest of the family about it. I just know how it's been making me feel, but... I guess I haven't noticed anyone else saying anything. Maybe they don't see it the same way, or maybe they just don't want to get involved. I really don't know.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your husband's sister is actually trying to push you out of the family business decisions?\n\nPatient: Yes, absolutely. Like I said a minute ago, that's exactly what she's doing. She wants control of the money, and she wants me out of the decisions. People try to smooth it over and act like she's just helping out with the business, but... well, I know what I've been seeing for over a year now. I'm not making it up.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your belief about your husband's sister is something that might not be true, or is it something you're absolutely certain about?\n\nPatient: I am absolutely certain. I mean, like I keep saying, I know what I've been seeing for more than a year now. Folks can try to smooth it over all they want, but... it doesn't change what's happening. She is working that family business just so she can get control of the money and push me out of the decisions. I don't have any doubts about it at all.\n\nDoctor: Has your belief about your husband's sister trying to push you out of the family business decisions ever changed since you first started thinking it?\n\nPatient: No, it hasn't changed. If anything, I'm just more sure of it now. Like I said, I know what I've been seeing for more than a year now. She's been working this family business just so she can get control of the money and push me out of the decisions. No matter how folks try to smooth it over, I know what's going on.\n\nDoctor: Has your belief about your husband's sister affected your daily activities or relationships outside of the family business?\n\nPatient: No, not really. My regular day-to-day stuff is fine. I mean, I still keep up with my quilting group, and choir practice... I do my GED lessons and go on my senior walks just like I always have. It hasn't stopped my normal life. \n\nMostly, this whole thing has just changed how much I trust my husband's sister, and... well, which family gatherings I actually feel comfortable going to now. I just stay away if I know she's going to be causing trouble. But outside of that, everything else is normal.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever thought that your husband's sister might not actually be trying to push you out of the family business decisions?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't. Like I've said, no matter how much folks try to smooth it over, I know what I've been seeing for more than a year now. I haven't ever thought otherwise because it's just what's happening.\n\nDoctor: Do you think there's any evidence that could change your mind about your husband's sister trying to push you out of the family business decisions?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. I mean, I came here to talk to you because of it, but... there's no evidence that could change my mind. My husband's sister has been working this family business so she can get control of the money and push me out of decisions. And no matter how folks try to smooth it over, I know what I've been seeing for more than a year now. It's just the truth of what's happening.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever considered that your husband's sister might actually care about the family business and is not trying to push you out?\n\nPatient: Doctor, I feel like I just keep saying the same thing to you, but no, I haven't. \n\nPeople can try to smooth it over all they want and say she means well, but I've been watching this happen for over a year now. I haven't gone around asking the rest of the family about it. I just know how it's been making me feel, but... I guess I haven't noticed anyone else saying anything. Maybe they don't see it the same way, or maybe they just don't want to get involved. Like I said a minute ago, that's exactly what she's doing. She wants control of the money, and she wants me out of the decisions. People try to smooth it over and act like she's just helping out with the business, but... well, I know what I've been seeing for over a year now. I'm not making it up.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your belief about your husband's sister is something that might not be true, or is it something you're absolutely certain about?\n\nPatient: I am absolutely certain. I mean, like I keep saying, I know what I've been seeing for more than a year now. Folks can try to smooth it over all they want, but... it doesn't change what's happening. She is working that family business just so she can get control of the money and push me out of the decisions. I don't have any doubts about it at all.\n\nDoctor: Has your belief about your husband's sister trying to push you out of the family business decisions ever changed since you first started thinking it?\n\nPatient: No, it hasn't changed. She's been working this family business just so she can get control of the money and push me out of the decisions. No matter how folks try to smooth it over, I know what's going on.\n\nDoctor: Has your belief about your husband's sister affected your daily activities or relationships outside of the family business?\n\nPatient: No, not really. It hasn't stopped my normal life. \n\nMostly, this whole thing has just changed how much I trust my husband's sister, and... well, which family gatherings I actually feel comfortable going to now. I just stay away if I know she's going to be causing trouble. Like I've said, no matter how much folks try to smooth it over, I know what I've been seeing for more than a year now. I haven't ever thought otherwise because it's just what's happening.\n\nDoctor: Do you think there's any evidence that could change your mind about your husband's sister trying to push you out of the family business decisions?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. I mean, I came here to talk to you because of it, but... there's no evidence that could change my mind. My husband's sister has been working this family business so she can get control of the money and push me out of decisions. And no matter how folks try to smooth it over, I know what I've been seeing for more than a year now. It's just the truth of what's happening.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever considered that your husband's sister might actually care about the family business and is not trying to push you out?\n\nPatient: Doctor, I feel like I just keep saying the same thing to you, but no, I haven't. \n\nPeople can try to smooth it over all they want and say she means well, but I've been watching this happen for over a year now. I know what I'm seeing. She's just working the family business so she can get total control of the money and push me out of making any decisions.
